'';\r\n      const prefix = 'Bearer ';\r\n      if (!auth.startsWith(prefix) || auth.slice(prefix.length) !== this.config.apiToken) {\r\n        this.writeJSON(res, 401, { error: 'Invalid or missing Bearer token' });\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      return next(req, res);\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // ===== Endpoint Handlers =====\r\n\r\n  private health(_req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): void {\r\n    const stats = this.config.getStats?.() ?? { totalTasks: 0, runningTasks: 0 } as Partial<AdminPoolStats>;\r\n    this.writeJSON(res, 200, {\r\n      status: 'healthy',\r\n      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n      tasks: stats.totalTasks ?? 0,\r\n      running: stats.runningTasks ?? 0,\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private listAgents(_req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): void {\r\n    const agents = this.config.getAgents?.() ?? {};\r\n    this.writeJSON(res, 200, agents);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private getAgent(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): void {\r\n    const id = (req.url ?? e.condition);\r\n          if (fallbackEdges.length === 0) {\r\n            return { output: '', nodeResults: results, totalDuration: Date.now() - startTime, success: false };\r\n          }\r\n };\r\n          currentInput = output;\r\n        }\r\n      } catch (err) {\r\n        results[nodeId] = { nodeId, output: '', status: 'failed', duration: Date.now() - nodeStart, error: (err as Error).message };\r\n        return { output: '', nodeResults: results, totalDuration: Date.now() - startTime, success: false };\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      completed.add(nodeId);\r\n\r\n      // Find next nodes based on edges\r\n      const nextEdges = this.edges.filter((e) => e.from === nodeId);\r\n      for (const edge of nextEdges) {\r\n        if (edge.condition && !edge.condition(currentInput)) {\r\n          continue; // Skip if condition not met\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // Find final output (last completed node)\r\n    const lastCompleted = order.filter((id) => results[id]?.status === 'completed').pop();\r\n    const output = lastCompleted ? results[lastCompleted].output : '';\r\n\r\n    return {\r\n      output,\r\n      nodeResults: results,\r\n      totalDuration: Date.now() - startTime,\r\n      success: true,\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  private async executeWithTimeout(\r\n    node: DAGNode,\r\n    input: string,\r\n    context: topologicalSort(): string[] {\r\n    const inDegree: Map<string, number> = new Map();\r\n    const adjList: Map<string, string[]> = new Map();\r\n\r\n    for (const nodeId of this.nodes.keys()) {\r\n      inDegree.set(nodeId, 0);\r\n      adjList.set(nodeId, []);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    for (const edge of this.edges) {\r\n      if (!adjList.has(edge.from)) adjList.set(edge.from, []);\r\n      adjList.get(edge.from)!.push(edge.to);\r\n      inDegree.set(edge.to, (inDegree.get(edge.to) ?? 0) + 1);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const queue: string[] = [];\r\n    for (const [id, deg] of inDegree) {\r\n      if (deg === 0) queue.push(id);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const result: string[] = [];\r\n    while (queue.length > 0) {\r\n      const current = queue.shift()!;\r\n      result.push(current);\r\n 3;\r\n    let currentTopic = topic;\r\n\r\n    for (let round = 0; round < maxRounds; round++) {\r\n      for (const agent of this.config.agents) {\r\n        const prompt = round === 0\r\n          ? a.load - b.load);\r\n        workerId = availableWorkers[0]!.id;\r\n        break;\r\n\r\n      case 'random':\r\n        workerId = availableWorkers[Math.floor(Math.random() * availableWorkers.length)]!.id;\r\n        break;\r\n\r\n      default:\r\n        workerId = availableWorkers[0]!.id;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const task = this.queue.shift()!;\r\n    task.assignedTo =
